\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{memcg\_memory\_event}{memcg\_memory\_event}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{memcontrol_8h_accc81c9064a67a03bbf66102c258f57e} 
enum \doxymbox{\hyperlink{memcontrol_8h_accc81c9064a67a03bbf66102c258f57e}{memcg\+\_\+memory\+\_\+event}}}

Enumeration Description:\+ This enumeration lists all memory events that can be triggered for a cgroup. Events include low memory, high memory, max limit reached, OOM (out-\/of-\/memory) events, OOM kills, swap high, swap max, swap failures, and socket throttling. These events are used for monitoring and alerting. \begin{DoxyEnumFields}[2]{Enumerator}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{memcg\_stat\_item}{memcg\_stat\_item}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{memcontrol_8h_a2a914b22e5ebe6947ef2f132956ba129} 
enum \doxymbox{\hyperlink{memcontrol_8h_a2a914b22e5ebe6947ef2f132956ba129}{memcg\+\_\+stat\+\_\+item}}}

Cgroup-\/specific page state, on top of universal node page state

Enumeration Description:\+ This enumeration lists all memory cgroup statistics items. It extends the node page state items with cgroup-\/specific counters like swap usage, socket memory, percpu memory, vmalloc memory, kernel memory, and zswap usage. These are used to track various types of memory usage per cgroup. \begin{DoxyEnumFields}[2]{Enumerator}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{memcontrol_8h_ab52d480df31b7b59a023c3c20d1eab1b} 
enum \doxymbox{\hyperlink{memcontrol_8h_ab52d480df31b7b59a023c3c20d1eab1b}{objext\+\_\+flags}}}

Enumeration Description:\+ These flags are used for slab object extensions. OBJEXTS\+\_\+\+ALLOC\+\_\+\+FAIL indicates that allocation of the \doxylink{structslabobj__ext}{slabobj\+\_\+ext} vector failed. Other flags are reserved for future use. These are used when tracking per-\/object memory accounting. \begin{DoxyEnumFields}[2]{Enumerator}
\raisebox{\heightof{T}}[0pt][0pt]{\index{OBJEXTS\_ALLOC\_FAIL@{OBJEXTS\_ALLOC\_FAIL}!memcontrol.h@{memcontrol.h}}

OBJEXTS\+\_\+\+ALLOC\+\_\+\+FAIL&Use bit 0 with zero other bits to signal that \doxylink{structslabobj__ext}{slabobj\+\_\+ext} vector failed to allocate. The same bit 0 with valid upper bits means MEMCG\+\_\+\+DATA\+\_\+\+OBJEXTS. \\
\hline

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{page\_memcg\_data\_flags}{page\_memcg\_data\_flags}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{memcontrol_8h_a529443b0e07b7c1fee9ece2042e70e7e} 
enum \doxymbox{\hyperlink{memcontrol_8h_a529443b0e07b7c1fee9ece2042e70e7e}{page\+\_\+memcg\+\_\+data\+\_\+flags}}}

Enumeration Description:\+ These flags are stored in the page\textquotesingle{}s memcg\+\_\+data field. MEMCG\+\_\+\+DATA\+\_\+\+OBJEXTS indicates the page has an \doxylink{structslabobj__ext}{slabobj\+\_\+ext} vector. MEMCG\+\_\+\+DATA\+\_\+\+KMEM indicates the page has been accounted as a non-\/slab kernel page. These flags help determine what type of memory accounting applies to a page. \begin{DoxyEnumFields}[2]{Enumerator}

MEMCG\+\_\+\+DATA\+\_\+\+OBJEXTS&page-\/\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}memcg\+\_\+data is a pointer to an \doxylink{structslabobj__ext}{slabobj\+\_\+ext} vector \\

\+\_\+\+\_\+folio\+\_\+memcg -\/ Get the memory cgroup associated with a non-\/kmem folio @folio:\+ Pointer to the folio.

Returns a pointer to the memory cgroup associated with the folio, or NULL. This function assumes that the folio is known to have a proper memory cgroup pointer. It\textquotesingle{}s not safe to call this function against some type of folios, e.\+g. slab folios or ex-\/slab folios or kmem folios.

Function Description:\+ Gets the memory cgroup associated with a non-\/kmem folio. It extracts the memcg pointer from folio-\/\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}memcg\+\_\+data after verifying the folio is not a slab folio and does not have OBJEXTS or KMEM flags. This is an internal function used by \doxylink{memcontrol_8h_a3f84ec97f7c4b5bbaa725fe9590de01b}{folio\+\_\+memcg()}.

\+\_\+\+\_\+folio\+\_\+objcg -\/ get the object cgroup associated with a kmem folio. @folio:\+ Pointer to the folio.

Returns a pointer to the object cgroup associated with the folio, or NULL. This function assumes that the folio is known to have a proper object cgroup pointer. It\textquotesingle{}s not safe to call this function against some type of folios, e.\+g. slab folios or ex-\/slab folios or LRU folios.

Function Description:\+ Gets the object cgroup associated with a kmem folio. It extracts the objcg pointer from folio-\/\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}memcg\+\_\+data after verifying the KMEM flag is set. This is an internal function used by \doxylink{memcontrol_8h_a3f84ec97f7c4b5bbaa725fe9590de01b}{folio\+\_\+memcg()} for kmem folios.

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{memcontrol_8h_a3f84ec97f7c4b5bbaa725fe9590de01b} 
struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structmem__cgroup}{mem\+\_\+cgroup}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} folio\+\_\+memcg (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct folio \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{folio}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [static]}}

folio\+\_\+memcg -\/ Get the memory cgroup associated with a folio. @folio:\+ Pointer to the folio.

Returns a pointer to the memory cgroup associated with the folio, or NULL. This function assumes that the folio is known to have a proper memory cgroup pointer. It\textquotesingle{}s not safe to call this function against some type of folios, e.\+g. slab folios or ex-\/slab folios.

For a non-\/kmem folio any of the following ensures folio and memcg binding stability:\+


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item the folio lock
\item LRU isolation
\item exclusive reference
\end{DoxyItemize}

For a kmem folio a caller should hold an rcu read lock to protect memcg associated with a kmem folio from being released.

Function Description:\+ Returns the memory cgroup associated with a folio. For kmem folios, it gets the objcg and returns its memcg. For non-\/kmem folios, it returns the memcg directly. This function assumes the folio has a proper memory cgroup pointer and is not a slab folio.

folio\+\_\+memcg\+\_\+check -\/ Get the memory cgroup associated with a folio. @folio:\+ Pointer to the folio.

Returns a pointer to the memory cgroup associated with the folio, or NULL. This function unlike \doxylink{memcontrol_8h_a3f84ec97f7c4b5bbaa725fe9590de01b}{folio\+\_\+memcg()} can take any folio as an argument. It has to be used in cases when it\textquotesingle{}s not known if a folio has an associated memory cgroup pointer or an object cgroups vector or an object cgroup.

For a non-\/kmem folio any of the following ensures folio and memcg binding stability:\+


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item the folio lock
\item LRU isolation
\item exclusive reference
\end{DoxyItemize}

For a kmem folio a caller should hold an rcu read lock to protect memcg associated with a kmem folio from being released.

Function Description:\+ Safely gets the memory cgroup associated with any folio. Unlike \doxylink{memcontrol_8h_a3f84ec97f7c4b5bbaa725fe9590de01b}{folio\+\_\+memcg()}, this function can handle slab folios, folios with OBJEXTS, and folios with KMEM flags. It uses READ\+\_\+\+ONCE() for safe access and returns NULL if the folio has OBJEXTS.

bool folio\+\_\+memcg\+\_\+kmem (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct folio \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{folio}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [static]}}

folio\+\_\+memcg\+\_\+kmem -\/ Check if the folio has the memcg\+\_\+kmem flag set. @folio:\+ Pointer to the folio.

Checks if the folio has Memcg\+Kmem flag set. The caller must ensure that the folio has an associated memory cgroup. It\textquotesingle{}s not safe to call this function against some types of folios, e.\+g. slab folios.

Function Description:\+ Checks if a folio has the memcg\+\_\+kmem flag set. This indicates the folio is a kernel memory folio charged to an object cgroup. The caller must ensure the folio has an associated memory cgroup.

get\+\_\+mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+from\+\_\+mm:\+ Obtain a reference on given mm\+\_\+struct\textquotesingle{}s memcg. @mm:\+ mm from which memcg should be extracted. It can be NULL.

Obtain a reference on mm-\/\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}memcg and returns it if successful. If mm is NULL, then the memcg is chosen as follows:\+ 1) The active memcg, if set. 2) current-\/\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}mm-\/\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}memcg, if available 3) root memcg If \doxylink{structmem__cgroup}{mem\+\_\+cgroup} is disabled, NULL is returned.

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{mem\_cgroup\_charge()}{mem\_cgroup\_charge()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{memcontrol_8h_a6e66d85ef3bc036f0554a049b27411e7} 
int mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+charge (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct folio \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{folio}{, }\item[{struct mm\+\_\+struct \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{mm}{, }\item[{gfp\+\_\+t}]{gfp}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [static]}}

mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+charge -\/ Charge a newly allocated folio to a cgroup. @folio:\+ Folio to charge. @mm:\+ mm context of the allocating task. @gfp:\+ Reclaim mode.

Try to charge @folio to the memcg that @mm belongs to, reclaiming pages according to @gfp if necessary. If @mm is NULL, try to charge to the active memcg.

Do not use this for folios allocated for swapin.

Return:\+ 0 on success. Otherwise, an error code is returned.

Function Description:\+ Charges a newly allocated folio to a memory cgroup. It tries to charge the folio to the memcg that the mm belongs to, reclaiming pages if necessary. Returns 0 on success or an error code. This is the main function for charging folios.

int mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+charge\+\_\+hugetlb (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct folio \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{folio}{, }\item[{gfp\+\_\+t}]{gfp}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+charge\+\_\+hugetlb -\/ charge the memcg for a hugetlb folio @folio:\+ folio being charged @gfp:\+ reclaim mode

This function is called when allocating a huge page folio, after the page has already been obtained and charged to the appropriate hugetlb cgroup controller (if it is enabled).

Returns ENOMEM if the memcg is already full. Returns 0 if either the charge was successful, or if we skip the charging.

If memcg is bound to the default hierarchy, css of the memcg associated with @folio is returned. The returned css remains associated with @folio until it is released.

If memcg is bound to a traditional hierarchy, the css of root\+\_\+mem\+\_\+cgroup is returned.

struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structmem__cgroup}{mem\+\_\+cgroup}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+get\+\_\+oom\+\_\+group (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct task\+\_\+struct \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{victim}{, }\item[{struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structmem__cgroup}{mem\+\_\+cgroup}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{oom\+\_\+domain}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+get\+\_\+oom\+\_\+group -\/ get a memory cgroup to clean up after OOM @victim:\+ task to be killed by the OOM killer @oom\+\_\+domain:\+ memcg in case of memcg OOM, NULL in case of system-\/wide OOM

Returns a pointer to a memory cgroup, which has to be cleaned up by killing all belonging OOM-\/killable tasks.

Caller has to call \doxylink{memcontrol_8h_a62c9264078ac378e5a5c70d94bd52d32}{mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+put()} on the returned non-\/\+NULL memcg.

struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structmem__cgroup}{mem\+\_\+cgroup}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+iter (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structmem__cgroup}{mem\+\_\+cgroup}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{root}{, }\item[{struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structmem__cgroup}{mem\+\_\+cgroup}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{prev}{, }\item[{struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structmem__cgroup__reclaim__cookie}{mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+reclaim\+\_\+cookie}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{reclaim}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+iter -\/ iterate over memory cgroup hierarchy @root:\+ hierarchy root @prev:\+ previously returned memcg, NULL on first invocation @reclaim:\+ cookie for shared reclaim walks, NULL for full walks

Returns references to children of the hierarchy below @root, or @root itself, or NULL after a full round-\/trip.

Caller must pass the return value in @prev on subsequent invocations for reference counting, or use \doxylink{memcontrol_8c_a9d0260b0dca7b94839c41a75ce96e596}{mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+iter\+\_\+break()} to cancel a hierarchy walk before the round-\/trip is complete.

Reclaimers can specify a node in @reclaim to divide up the memcgs in the hierarchy among all concurrent reclaimers operating on the same node. 

Definition at line \doxymbox{\hyperlink{memcontrol_8c_source_l01021}{1021}} of file \doxymbox{\hyperlink{memcontrol_8c_source}{memcontrol.\+c}}.

Returns the lru list vector holding pages for a given @memcg \& @pgdat combination. This can be the node lruvec, if the memory controller is disabled.

Function Description:\+ Returns the lru list vector for a memcg and node combination. It gets the per-\/node memcg data and returns its lruvec. This is used to get the correct LRU list for a memcg on a specific node.

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{memcontrol_8h_a3da1ba73ef841faed60e57d883bdbf54} 
void mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+migrate (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct folio \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{old}{, }\item[{struct folio \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{new}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+migrate -\/ Transfer the memcg data from the old to the new folio. @old:\+ Currently circulating folio. @new:\+ Replacement folio.

Transfer the memcg data from the old folio to the new folio for migration. The old folio\textquotesingle{}s data info will be cleared. Note that the memory counters will remain unchanged throughout the process.

Both folios must be locked, @new-\/\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}mapping must be set up.

\Hypertarget{memcontrol_8h_ac8996efe422805334922aa83951e1dcf}\index{memcontrol.h@{memcontrol.h}!mem\_cgroup\_protection@{mem\_cgroup\_protection}}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{mem\_cgroup\_protection()}{mem\_cgroup\_protection()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{memcontrol_8h_ac8996efe422805334922aa83951e1dcf} 
void mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+protection (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structmem__cgroup}{mem\+\_\+cgroup}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{root}{, }\item[{struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structmem__cgroup}{mem\+\_\+cgroup}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{memcg}{, }\item[{unsigned long \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{min}{, }\item[{unsigned long \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{low}{, }\item[{unsigned long \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{usage}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [static]}}

Function Description:\+ Gets the memory protection values for a cgroup. It reads the effective min and low values from the memcg\textquotesingle{}s memory page counter. These values are used to determine how much memory the cgroup is protected from reclaim. There is no reclaim protection applied to a targeted reclaim. We are special casing this specific case here because mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+calculate\+\_\+protection is not robust enough to keep the protection invariant for calculated effective values for parallel reclaimers with different reclaim target. This is especially a problem for tail memcgs (as they have pages on LRU) which would want to have effective values 0 for targeted reclaim but a different value for external reclaim.

Example Let\textquotesingle{}s have global and A\textquotesingle{}s reclaim in parallel:\+ \texorpdfstring{$\vert$}{|} A (low=2G, usage = 3G, max = 3G, children\+\_\+low\+\_\+usage = 1.\+5G) \texorpdfstring{$\vert$}{|}\textbackslash{} \texorpdfstring{$\vert$}{|} C (low = 1G, usage = 2.\+5G) B (low = 1G, usage = 0.\+5G)

For the global reclaim A.\+elow = A.\+low B.\+elow = min(B.\+usage, B.\+low) because children\+\_\+low\+\_\+usage \texorpdfstring{$<$}{<}= A.\+elow C.\+elow = min(C.\+usage, C.\+low)

With the effective values resetting we have A reclaim A.\+elow = 0 B.\+elow = B.\+low C.\+elow = C.\+low

If the global reclaim races with A\textquotesingle{}s reclaim then B.\+elow = C.\+elow = 0 because children\+\_\+low\+\_\+usage \texorpdfstring{$>$}{>} A.\+elow) is possible and reclaiming B would be violating the protection.

mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+update\+\_\+lru\+\_\+size -\/ account for adding or removing an lru page @lruvec:\+ \doxylink{structmem__cgroup}{mem\+\_\+cgroup} per zone lru vector @lru:\+ index of lru list the page is sitting on @zid:\+ zone id of the accounted pages @nr\+\_\+pages:\+ positive when adding or negative when removing

This function must be called under lru\+\_\+lock, just before a page is added to or just after a page is removed from an lru list.

\index{mem\_cgroup\_wb\_stats@{mem\_cgroup\_wb\_stats}!memcontrol.h@{memcontrol.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{mem\_cgroup\_wb\_stats()}{mem\_cgroup\_wb\_stats()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{memcontrol_8h_a7bc22f3f28d17ceecb4feb5b2db15d93} 
void mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+wb\+\_\+stats (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct bdi\+\_\+writeback \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{wb}{, }\item[{unsigned long \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{pfilepages}{, }\item[{unsigned long \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{pheadroom}{, }\item[{unsigned long \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{pdirty}{, }\item[{unsigned long \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{pwriteback}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+wb\+\_\+stats -\/ retrieve writeback related stats from its memcg @wb:\+ bdi\+\_\+writeback in question @pfilepages:\+ out parameter for number of file pages @pheadroom:\+ out parameter for number of allocatable pages according to memcg @pdirty:\+ out parameter for number of dirty pages @pwriteback:\+ out parameter for number of pages under writeback

Determine the numbers of file, headroom, dirty, and writeback pages in @wb\textquotesingle{}s memcg. File, dirty and writeback are self-\/explanatory. Headroom is a bit more involved.

A memcg\textquotesingle{}s headroom is "{}min(max, high) -\/ used"{}. In the hierarchy, the headroom is calculated as the lowest headroom of itself and the ancestors. Note that this doesn\textquotesingle{}t consider the actual amount of available memory in the system. The caller should further cap \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}@pheadroom accordingly.

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{memcontrol_8h_afb088148a129989b51ab511d22e48dfd} 
bool obj\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+may\+\_\+zswap (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structobj__cgroup}{obj\+\_\+cgroup}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{objcg}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

obj\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+may\+\_\+zswap -\/ check if this cgroup can zswap @objcg:\+ the object cgroup

Check if the hierarchical zswap limit has been reached.

This doesn\textquotesingle{}t check for specific headroom, and it is not atomic either. But with zswap, the size of the allocation is only known once compression has occurred, and this optimistic pre-\/check avoids spending cycles on compression when there is already no room left or zswap is disabled altogether somewhere in the hierarchy.

After the initialization objcg-\/\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}memcg is always pointing at a valid memcg, but can be atomically swapped to the parent memcg.

The caller must ensure that the returned memcg won\textquotesingle{}t be released.

Function Description:\+ Returns the memory cgroup associated with an object cgroup. It reads the memcg pointer from the objcg structure. The caller must ensure RCU read lock is held or cgroup\+\_\+mutex is locked to prevent the memcg from being released.

parent\+\_\+mem\+\_\+cgroup -\/ find the accounting parent of a memcg @memcg:\+ memcg whose parent to find

Returns the parent memcg, or NULL if this is the root.

Function Description:\+ Returns the parent of a memory cgroup. It gets the parent css from the memcg\textquotesingle{}s css and converts it to a memcg. Returns NULL if the memcg is the root.
